  274 | 
  275 | /** What a call did: answered, dropped by the browser, or never sent. */
  276 | type CallOutcome = { response: Response } | { dropped: string; request: Request } | null;
  277 | 
  278 | /** Fails naming what the service said, rather than what the editor did not do. */
  279 | async function expectAccepted(page: Page, call: Promise<CallOutcome>, what: string): Promise<void> {
  280 |   const outcome = await call;
  281 | 
  282 |   if (outcome === null) {
  283 |     // The app refused before sending: no credits shows a notice and stops here.
  284 |     throw new Error(`${what} sent no request${await whatTheAppSaid(page)}.${alsoSeen(page)}`);
  285 |   }
  286 | 
  287 |   if ('dropped' in outcome) {
  288 |     // The app catches this one and puts it in a notification.
  289 |     throw new Error(
  290 |       `${what} never reached the service: ${outcome.dropped}` +
  291 |         `${await whatTheAppSaid(page)}.${alsoSeen(page)}`
  292 |     );
  293 |   }
  294 | 
  295 |   // waitForResponse observes headers. The core API client decodes the body before
  296 |   // resolving api.post<string>(), so wait for that body before checking UI state.
  297 |   const completionError = await outcome.response.finished();
  298 | 
  299 |   if (completionError) {
  300 |     throw new Error(`${what} response did not finish: ${completionError}`);
  301 |   }
  302 | 
  303 |   if (!outcome.response.ok()) {
  304 |     const said = await outcome.response
  305 |       .text()
  306 |       .then((body) => body.replaceAll(/\s+/g, ' ').trim())
  307 |       .catch(() => '');
  308 |     throw new Error(
  309 |       `${what} was refused: ${outcome.response.status()} ${outcome.response.url()} ${said}`
  310 |     );
  311 |   }
  312 | }
  313 | 
  314 | async function whatTheAppSaid(page: Page): Promise<string> {
  315 |   const notice = lowCredits(page)
  316 |     .notice.or(page.getByRole('alert').filter({ hasText: /\S/ }))
  317 |     .first();
  318 | 
  319 |   const said = await notice
  320 |     .innerText()
  321 |     .then((text) => text.trim().replaceAll(/\s+/g, ' '))
  322 |     .catch(() => '');
  323 | 
  324 |   return said === '' ? '' : `, and said "${said}"`;
  325 | }
  326 | 
  327 | function alsoSeen(page: Page): string {
  328 |   const problems = pageProblems(page);
  329 |   return problems.length === 0 ? '' : `\nThe page also saw:\n${problems.join('\n')}`;
  330 | }
  331 | 
  332 | /**
  333 |  * Moves to one of the editor's tabs.
  334 |  *
  335 |  * The pointer is left wherever the last click landed, and a tooltip that opens
  336 |  * under it is drawn into a popper that can cover the tab bar and swallow every
  337 |  * click until the action gives up — thirty seconds spent being told that
  338 |  * "virtual" intercepts pointer events. Escape closes whatever is showing, and
  339 |  * what it dismissed stays shut until the pointer leaves and comes back, which
  340 |  * the click itself does.
  341 |  */
  342 | async function openTab(
  343 |   page: Page,
  344 |   editor: ReturnType<typeof scanConfigEditor>,
  345 |   id: string
  346 | ): Promise<void> {
  347 |   await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
  348 |   await editor.tab(id).click(NO_NAVIGATION);
  349 | }
  350 | 
  351 | /**
  352 |  * Waits for a coordinate to stop moving.
  353 |  *
  354 |  * The wait stays on the page it launched from. A campaign is held in the
  355 |  * editor's own state rather than in the URL, so reloading loses it: the page
  356 |  * comes back on the configuration tab with no coordinates to read. That rules
  357 |  * out the usual trick of polling a fresh page, and it is why a run longer than
  358 |  * the `@slow` job can hold has to be picked up from the Workflows activity
  359 |  * table instead of followed from here.
  360 |  *
  361 |  * `toPass` rather than a single long `toHaveText`, so a run that ends in
  362 |  * "error" is reported as that instead of as a timeout with nothing to say.
  363 |  */
  364 | export async function waitForCampaign(status: Locator, minutes: number): Promise<void> {
  365 |   await expect(async () => {
  366 |     const text = (await status.innerText()).trim();
  367 |     expect(text, `The campaign is still "${text}".`).toMatch(SETTLED);
> 368 |   }).toPass({ timeout: minutes * 60_000, intervals: [POLL_INTERVAL] });
      |     ^ Error: The campaign is still "Running".
  369 | }
  370 |
